Finished Output\n    (19, 24),  # Scrap And Rework\n    (26, 31),  # Rated Production Capacity\n]\n\n# Derived blocks\nSLACK_ROWS = (35, 40)  # per-plant slack rows\nSTATS_ROWS = (42, 47)  # summary statistics rows\nWEIGHTED_MEAN_ROW = 50\n\n\ndef a1_range(col1: str, row1: int, col2: str, row2: int) -> str:\n    return f\"${col1}${row1}:${col2}${row2}\"\n\n\ndef main() -> None:\n    wb = load_workbook(SRC_PATH)  # keep formulas/styles; do NOT use data_only\n    ws_task = wb[TASK_SHEET]\n\n    # Build absolute ranges for the INDEX/MATCH lookup.\n    data_values = f\"{DATA_SHEET}!{a1_range(DATA_FIRST_YEAR_COL, DATA_FIRST_DATA_ROW, DATA_LAST_YEAR_COL, DATA_LAST_DATA_ROW)}\"\n    data_row_keys = f\"{DATA_SHEET}!${DATA_SERIES_CODE_COL}${DATA_FIRST_DATA_ROW}:${DATA_SERIES_CODE_COL}${DATA_LAST_DATA_ROW}\"\n    data_col_keys = f\"{DATA_SHEET}!${DATA_FIRST_YEAR_COL}${DATA_YEAR_HEADER_ROW}:${DATA_LAST_YEAR_COL}${DATA_YEAR_HEADER_ROW}\"\n\n    # --- Step 1: lookup formulas into H:L for rows in the 3 blocks ---\n    for r1, r2 in LOOKUP_BLOCKS:\n        for r in range(r1, r2 + 1):\n            for c in range(ws_task[\"H1\"].column, ws_task[\"L1\"].column + 1):\n                col_letter = get_column_letter(c)\n                # --- Step 2: net production slack H35:L40 ---\n    # Map slack rows (35-40) to the corresponding rows in Step 1 blocks:\n    # Finished Output: rows 12-17  => slack_row - 23\n    # Scrap:          rows 19-24  => slack_row - 16\n    # --- Step 2b: statistics rows H42:L47 (match labels in column E) ---\n    label_to_kind = {\n        \"Minimum\": \"MIN\",\n        \"Maximum\": \"MAX\",\n        \"Median\": \"MEDIAN\",\n        \"Simple mean\": \"AVERAGE\",\n        \"25th percentile\": \"PERCENTILE_25\",\n        \"75th percentile\": \"PERCENTILE_75\",\n    }\n\n    stats_r1, stats_r2 = STATS_ROWS\n    for r in range(stats_r1, stats_r2 + 1):\n        label = ws_task[f\"E{r}\"].value\n        kind = label_to_kind.get(label)\n        if not kind:\n            raise ValueError(f\"Unexpected stats label at E{r}: {label!r}\")\n\n        for c in range(ws_task[\"H1\"].column, ws_task[\"L1\"].column + 1):\n            col_letter = get_column_letter(c)\n            slack_range = f\"{col_letter}{slack_r1}:{col_letter}{slack_r2}\"\n            if kind in {\"MIN\", \"MAX\", \"MEDIAN\", \"AVERAGE\"}:\n                formula = f\"={kind}({slack_range})\"\n            elif kind == \"PERCENTILE_25\":\n                formula = f\"=PERCENTILE({slack_range},0.25)\"\n            elif kind == \"PERCENTILE_75\":\n                formula = f\"=PERCENTILE({slack_range},0.75)\"\n            else:\n                raise AssertionError(kind)\n\n            ws_task[f\"{col_letter}{r}\"].value = formula\n\n    # --- Step 2: net production slack H35:L40 ---\n    # Map slack rows (35-40) to the corresponding rows in Step 1 blocks:\n    # Finished Output: rows 12-17  => slack_row - 23\n    # Scrap:          rows 19-24  => slack_row - 16\n    # Rated cap:      rows 26-31  => slack_row - 9\n    slack_r1, slack_r2 = SLACK_ROWS\n    for r in range(slack_r1, slack_r2 + 1):\n        fin_r = r - 23\n        scrap_r = r - 16\n        cap_r = r - 9\n        for c in range(ws_task[\"H1\"].column, ws_task[\"L1\"].column + 1):\n            col_letter = get_column_letter(c)\n            formula = f\"=({col_letter}{fin_r}-{col_letter}{scrap_r})/{col_letter}{cap_r}*100\"\n            ws_task[f\"{col_letter}{r}\"].value = formula\n\n    # --- Step 2b: statistics rows H42:L47 (match labels in column E) ---\n    label_to_kind = {\n        \"Minimum\": \"MIN\",\n        \"Maximum\": \"MAX\",\n        \"Median\": \"MEDIAN\",\n        \"Simple mean\": \"AVERAGE\",\n        \"25th percentile\": \"PERCENTILE_25\",\n        \"75th percentile\": \"PERCENTILE_75\",\n    }\n\n    stats_r1, stats_r2 = STATS_ROWS\n    for r in range(stats_r1, stats_r2 + 1):\n        label = ws_task[f\"E{r}\"].value\n        kind = label_to_kind.get(label)\n        if not kind:\n            raise ValueError(f\"Unexpected stats label at E{r}: {label!r}\")\n\n        for c in range(ws_task[\"H1\"].column, ws_task[\"L1\"].column + 1):\n            col_letter = get_column_letter(c)\n            slack_range = f\"{col_letter}{slack_r1}:{col_letter}{slack_r2}\"\n            if kind in {\"MIN\", \"MAX\", \"MEDIAN\", \"AVERAGE\"}:\n                formula = f\"={kind}({slack_range})\"\n            elif kind == \"PERCENTILE_25\":\n                formula = f\"=PERCENTILE({slack_range},0.25)\"\n            elif kind == \"PERCENTILE_75\":\n                formula = f\"=PERCENTILE({slack_range},0.75)\"\n            else:\n                raise AssertionError(kind)\n\n            ws_task[f\"{col_letter}{r}\"].value = formula\n\n    # --- Step 3: weighted mean in H50:L50 ---\n    r = WEIGHTED_MEAN_ROW\n    cap_r1, cap_r2 = (26, 31)\n    for c in range(ws_task[\"H1\"].column, ws_task[\"L1\"].column + 1):\n        col_letter = get_column_letter(c)\n        slack_range = f\"{col_letter}{slack_r1}:{col_letter}{slack_r2}\"\n        cap_range = f\"{col_letter}{cap_r1}:{col_letter}{cap_r2}\"\n        formula = f\"=SUMPRODUCT({slack_range},{cap_range})/SUM({cap_range})\"\n        ws_task[f\"{col_letter}{r}\"].value = formula\n\n    os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(OUT_PATH), exist_ok=True)\n    wb.save(OUT_PATH)\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null,"userModified":false}}
